langcurriculum › analogy, causality, planning, and programs › causal_language

#42 causal_language

causation vs correlation, decided by intervention

level 42section ivcausal_reasoningscientific_inductionabstraction
4.0 answers per episode
0.25 uniform-guess floor
0.28 constant-guess floor
100 samples below

What it generates

Correlation is made *useless* here: the mechanisms are deterministic, so in the observational trace every pair of variables in a component is perfectly (anti-)correlated. Only the interventional blocks separate the claims, and they separate them exactly — under do(u=0)/do(u=1), a variable moves iff u is its causal ancestor.

Difficulty axes

axislevel
ambiguity2
compositional_depth2
reasoning_depth4
world_complexity3
